### Slovenian (2026-10-22)

Leipzig's Slovenian news (2020, 100,000 sentences) and Wikipedia (2021, 100,000 sentences); sl-en and sl-ru (wty
2026.10.22) as the dictionaries.

- wty sl-en: 56,814 rows; 5,808 headwords with definitions, 48,678 form headwords, 39,804 of them with tonemic marks.
  Marks in form headwords: acute 28,136, caron 15,626 (č š ž, letters), inverted breve 7,155, dot below 3,695,
  circumflex 3,129, grave 1,672, macron 1,597 (ọ̄ ẹ̄ ā, not in the planned list; added), double grave 1,202. ł in 766
  forms and ə in 405 (dẹ́lał, pə̏s), which running text writes l and e. Headwords with definitions carry almost no
  stress (21); sl-ru has 73 headwords with an acute (Argentína, ponedéljek) and 6 forms with a Cyrillic е (srcе).
- Lemma names: 92.2% of sl-en's resolve as written, 93.2% without the marks; 97 form-of items name a lemma with ə or ł
  (várən), which only the replacements would resolve.
- Coverage of lowercase words (sl-en and sl-ru): news 1,570,855 words, 50.4% as written, the stress keys 14.6% more,
  the caron keys 0.08% more, 34.9% missing; Wikipedia 1,370,738 words: 49.1%, 16.1%, 0.06%, 34.7%. Missing most
  often: ob, naj, sicer, saj, ter, zato, proti, glede, namreč, treba. FreeDict slv-eng (5,511 headwords, GPL-3.0+)
  would add 3.4% (ob, ter, saj, zato, proti).
- Words with č š ž: 182,770 of 2,941,593 lowercase words (6%). Written without the caron, 164,217 are found through
  the keys and 18,552 have a bare spelling that is a word itself, which the exact-first order puts first (se/še
  13,807, sest/šest 606, zeli/želi 527, soli/šoli 399). Text without the caron that only the keys find: zdi → ždí
  386, krize → kríže 243, zanje → žánje 174, zame → žáme 104, stal → štál 102 (false), roza → roža 19, ze → že 7
  (right).
- ć 2,051 and Ć 22 times, đ 57 and Đ 125, all in names (Dončić, Đoković); ć keeps its acute.
- Abbreviations (lowercase / capital / end): npr. 246 / 28 / 258; t. i. 182 / 33 / 17, t.i. 33 / 5 / 2; tj. 56 / 3 /
  34; oz. 400 / 2 / 367; prof. 43 / 0 / 36; g. 5 / 57 / 5; št. 165 / 0 / 368 (3 before a digit); pr. 460 / 0 / 24;
  n. 518 / 15 / 2 (n. št.); pr. n. št. 151 / 0 / 212; n. št. 162 / 0 / 241; stol. 23 / 0 / 17; ur. 4 / 0 / 85 (ur,
  hours); itd. 9 / 0 / 107, ipd. 3 / 0 / 63, idr. 2 / 0 / 27 (left to end the sentence); ga. none but 9 ends (the
  pronoun); sv. 6 / 5 / 479 (the splitter's cuts after cerkev sv.); d.o.o. 5 / 4 / 2. Leipzig's splitter cuts after
  many abbreviations, so the ends include its cuts.
- Numbers with a period: one or two digits before a lowercase word 10,328, before a capital 425 (1. SNL, the league),
  ending a sentence 1,175; four digits 106 before a lowercase word, none before a capital. Roman numerals: 683
  lowercase, 154 capital, 318 at an end.
- A single capital letter with a period (initials): 764 before a capital, 340 lowercase, 93 at an end.
- Quotes: » 9,426 and « 9,172 (»…« 6,317 pairs), " 11,063, “ 374, ” 245, „ 130; „…“ 114 pairs, «…» 662. Paired: »…«
  and „…“.
- Audio: Lingua Libre Q9063 has 6 of 20 common words (voda, mati, knjiga among them), Wiktionary 2.
- Fonts: every catalog Latin font has č š ž.
- The bundle: Yomitan's Czech descriptor has capitalization only, so sl.js (Czech's under Slovenian's code) reads text
  as cs.js does; Slovenian needs no rules of its own (sl-en lists 48,678 forms).

### Esperanto (2026-10-28)

Leipzig's Esperanto news (2017, 100,000 sentences) and Wikipedia (2021, 100,000 sentences); eo-en and eo-ru (wty
2026.10.22) and ESPDIC as the dictionaries.

- wty eo-en: 317,333 rows; 19,748 headwords with definitions, 181,754 form headwords; rules field v 142,197, n
  97,210, adj 64,862, adv 8,775. Marks in headwords: circumflex 35,129, breve 6,124 (acute 2). eo-ru: 37,053 rows,
  11,752 with definitions, 25,269 forms; 18 headwords with ǔ (taǔgecoj), left as they are.
- Coverage of lowercase words: news 1,605,838 words, wty 88.3% as written, the keys without hats 0.05% more (859:
  pliigis → pliiĝis, gisis → ĝisis), ESPDIC 2.1% more, 9.5% missing; Wikipedia 1,278,503 words: 89.5%, 0.06%, 2.0%,
  8.4%, the x-system 4 words (konatigxis).
- Words with hats: 272,291 of 2,884,341 lowercase words (9.4%); 75,873 of them have a bare spelling that is a word
  itself (au/aŭ 15,147, ankau/ankaŭ 11,731, lau/laŭ 4,123, ci/ĉi 2,943, si/ŝi 2,708, igis/iĝis 2,510), which the
  exact-first order puts first.
- The x-system: 256 tokens with cx gx hx jx sx ux, nearly all foreign (Huxley 18, Bordeaux 7, aux 7, Guangxi 5);
  1,530 headwords contain such pairs (jxurnalo, an x-system form row). The h-system: 21 words (monahhejoj, egho),
  against 35 words with ch gh hh jh sh that are words or names as written (che, shi, chu, char): not read.
- Letters engines give for the hat letters, in typed text: Š 111, š 378, Č 77, č 286 (Czech names: Škoda, Čapek),
  ů 40, ű 14, ğ 18, ħ 3, ũ 2; ǔ 73 (aǔ, laǔ, kontraǔ: typed for ŭ).
- Apostrophes: 2,828 tokens; the elided o (dank'al 42, Dank'al 17, l' 59), English possessives and names (People's,
  O'Connor), and it'oj (34), Francio'n (22), which mark an ending on a foreign word.
- Abbreviations (lowercase / capital / end): ekz. 256 / 22 / 372 (9 before a digit); t.e. 194 / 19 / 5; t.n. 70 /
  48 / 24; i.a. 80 / 8 / 16; ĉ. 404 before a digit, 44 / 0 / 21; p. 352 before a digit, 6 / 9 / 5; vd. 11 / 0 / 6;
  kp. 7 / 0 / 10; resp. 28 / 0 / 24; ktp. 34 / 0 / 408; k.a. 2 / 5 / 6; a.K. 121 / 61 / 0; p.K. 42 / 23 / 0; jc.
  10 / 0 / 29; nask. 3 / 3 / 6 (14 before a year); prof. 0 / 0 / 15, St. 3 / 0 / 280 (the splitter's cuts after
  St. before a name). Leipzig's splitter cuts after many abbreviations, so the ends include its cuts. m. (metres,
  70 before a capital, 70 at an end) and km. (127 at an end) end the sentence; n-ro and s-ro are written with a
  hyphen and no period.
- Numbers with a period: one or two digits 46 before a lowercase word, 239 before a capital (list items, 1. Supra);
  ordinals take -a: 5,348 with -a, 2,263 with -an, 2,023 with -aj (1940-aj). No ordinal period.
- Quotes: “ 1,521, ” 864, „ 893, « 642, » 596, " 36,293; “…” 635 pairs, „…“ 684, „…” 97, «…» 492, »…« 182, ”…”
  140. Paired: “…”, „…“ and «…».
- Audio: Lingua Libre Q143 has 20 of 20 common words from 10 speakers, Wiktionary 20 (`Eo-`).
- Fonts: Atkinson Hyperlegible Next has none of ĉ ĝ ĥ ĵ ŝ ŭ; Literata, Inter, Lexend, PT Serif and PT Sans have all.
- Frequency (question 28): Leipzig's Wikipedia list (2021) has 162,366 lowercase words; ŝi ranks 46 and si 366,
  aŭ 22 and au 3,689, loĝantoj 126 and logantoj once, ĉu 177 and cu once. The news list (2017) has English
  passages (question 28), so the Wikipedia one is used.

### Albanian (2026-10-28)

Leipzig's Albanian Wikipedia (2021, 100,000 sentences) and news (2020, 100,000 sentences); wty sq-en and sq-ru
(2026.10.22) as the dictionaries.

- Abbreviations (before a lowercase word / a capital / a digit / at the end, Wikipedia; Leipzig's splitter cuts after
  many abbreviations, so the ends include its cuts): p.sh. 94 / 34 / 2 / 14; psh. 10 / 1 / 0 / 25; d.m.th. 42 / 0 / 2
  / 0; dmth. 10 / 2 / 1 / 19; nr. 1 / 0 / 35 / 87; fq. 0 / 0 / 7 / 63; shek. 6 / 18 / 2 / 332; v. 3 / 40 / 26 / 0;
  z. 2 / 120 / 2 / 4; dr. 0 / 22 / 0 / 95; prof. 15 / 0 / 0 / 64; zv. (news) 32 / 0 / 0 / 20. Before a lowercase word
  only: etj. 54 / 191 / 0 / 308 (news 13 / 31 / 0 / 76), p.e.s. 37 / 19 / 1 / 18, e.s. 17 / 7 / 0 / 4, km. 4 / 0 /
  0 / 66, kg. 0 / 0 / 0 / 20.
- Ordinals take an ending: a number before -të 671 times; no ordinal period.
- Quotes (Wikipedia / news): “…” 2,681 / 9,497 pairs, "…" 6,523 / 345, ”…” 521 / 1,122, «…» 87 / 36, „…“ 80 / 24,
  ‘…’ 92 / 1,062. Paired: “…” and «…».
- Elisions, Wikipedia: t' 1,146, t’ 745, s' 85, s’ 80, ç' 65, ç’ 58, m' 10; news: t’ 4,412, s’ 747, ç’ 198, t' 106,
  m’ 56, ç' 6. t' and m' are built beside the decision's s' and ç' (question 31).
- ě in typed text: 14 in Wikipedia, 1 in the news; Ë 1,022 and 1,178.
- Endings: the list of the plan's research; the built rules give the same hits on its three samples (613, 603 and 639
  tokens).
- Recognition (clean / hard / both, characters wrong): ML Kit 1.2 / 0.9 / 1.0%; Tesseract fast 2.3 / 9.5 / 5.9%;
  Paddle v5 and v6 0%; cloud recognition 0.0%; Tesseract best 4.3% on the emulator.
- Audio: Lingua Libre Q8748 has 3 of 20 common words, Wiktionary none.
- On the emulator: ML Kit reads Ç'është as Çështë, the apostrophe lost; çështje is a word of its own, so no OCR
  rule puts it back. Cloud recognition and PaddleOCR v5 keep it. All three read “…” around a short word as "…".
  sq-en has no kryesore and no kryesor; the fallback endings were seen on rëndësishme and ndodhet.

### Swahili (2026-10-28)

Leipzig's Swahili Wikipedia (2021, 100,000 sentences; the 100K news, newscrawl, web and community names return 404,
and only `swa_news_2020_30K` and `_10K` exist besides); wty sw-en (2026.10.22) as the dictionary.

- Abbreviations (before a lowercase word / a capital / a digit / at the end; Leipzig's splitter cuts after many
  abbreviations, so the ends include its cuts): k.m. 20 / 18 / 1 / 61; Bw. 1 / 0 / 0 / 71; Bi. 0 / 0 / 0 / 74; Mt.
  108 at the end; Dr. 1 / 0 / 0 / 46; Dk. 16, Dkt. 8, Nd. 7, Prof. 6, Mhe. 4 at the end; S.L.P. 1 before a digit;
  tar. 186 at the end (before a date: the splitter's cuts). Before a lowercase word only: n.k. 16 / 15 / 0 / 65; nk.
  1 / 0 / 0 / 10; K.K. 5 / 1 / 0 / 0; KK. 1 / 0 / 0 / 28; B.K. 1 / 0 / 0 / 0; BK. 11 at the end; uk. 0 / 0 / 8 / 61;
  km. 27 and kg. 7 at the end. t. (16 before a capital) is an initial, left out. The words most often before a period
  are sentence ends (humo 1,867, yake 980, huu 869).
- Ordinals are words: "ya tano", "wa kwanza" and the like 5,153 times; one or two digits with a period 34 before a
  lowercase word, 368 before a capital (list items). No ordinal period.
- Quotes: " 13,451, ' 2,847, “ 1,219, ” 1,216, ’ 790, ‘ 319, „ 42, « 32, » 32. Paired: “…”.
- ng' with an apostrophe: ' 174, ’ 34, ´ ` ʼ once each.
- Prefixes (sw-en, the 30,000 most frequent words weighted by count): 81.39% found as written, 8.86% more through the
  prefixes, 9.74% missing (iko, Kufuatana, imehesabiwa, ambapo, names, English). Two syllables after every tense and
  in the relative suffix lost right hits (akiwa, akaja, hakuwa, iliyo), so one-syllable stems stay allowed after
  -ki-, -ka-, the negative -ku- and -ja-, and before the relative suffix.
- Recognition (clean / hard / both, characters wrong): ML Kit 0.4 / 0.0 / 0.2%; Tesseract fast 0.7 / 0.7 / 0.7%;
  Paddle v5 and v6 0%; cloud recognition 0.0%.
- Audio: Wiktionary has 18 of 20 common words (`Sw-ke-…`), Lingua Libre Q7838 none.
- Catalog: wty sw-en 11,649 words, sw-ru 8,143, sw-zh 2,386 and sw-pl 2,061 are listed; sw-fr 1,397, sw-ko 665,
  sw-pt 661, sw-ja 513, sw-es 446, sw-tr 362, sw-de 117, sw-vi 97 and the transcription (1,282) stay under the line.

### Welsh (2026-10-28)

Leipzig's Welsh Wikipedia (2021, 100,000 sentences, `cym_wikipedia_2021_100K`, CC BY) for the exception list; wty
cy-en, cy-fr and cy-ru (2026.10.22) as the dictionaries.

- Exception list: `mutation-exceptions.mjs cy <sentences> 1000 wty-cy-en.zip wty-cy-fr.zip wty-cy-ru.zip`: 55
  candidates not attested (the English dictionary gives no rule result as the word's lemma), 28 own and common, 220
  left to the rule. Listed 94: the 55 but elwir (after a, the soft form of gelwir), the 28, and by hand yr, hun,
  gall, menywod, naw, du, dan, drwy, gol, rôl and English the, of. Left to the rule: lan (mostly glan after ar),
  bobl, ben, fod and the rest of the 220.
- Shares of tokens, without the list → with it: cy-en extra entries 34.10% → 12.46%, found by the rule alone 1.54%
  (unchanged); cy-fr 8.14% → 3.66%, 4.41% → 1.27%; cy-ru 1.20% → 0.03%, 4.45% → 4.10%.
- Rows: cy-en writes the soft rows `soft` (fara → b, a single letter: dropped), `soft mutation`, `nasal mutation`,
  `aspirate mutation`, and `alt-of` with `prothesis-h` (hysgol → ysgol); cy-fr `lenition`, `nasalization` and the
  reversed `unmutated` (te → the). Rows whose form does not end like the lemma's tail (the lemma without its first
  letter, or two before h): cy-en 727 of 20,532, cy-fr 1,488 of 3,875 (nghath → cathod; jip → tsip is right).
  Radical words have rows of their own (bara → para "soft"), which the rule's step reaches a second time: the
  engine drops such chains.
- Catalog: wty cy-en 17,501 words, cy-fr 3,817, cy-ru 2,887 and the transcription (16,756) are listed and ticked;
  cy-pl 1,303, cy-zh 1,108, cy-pt 905, cy-tr 544, cy-es 435, cy-ja 413, cy-de 150 and cy-vi 56 stay under the line.
- Recognition, audio, fonts: the plan's measurements (`plans/2026-10-19-group-5b.md`, Research).

### Irish (2026-10-28)

Leipzig's Irish news corpus (2014, 300,000 sentences, `gle_newscrawl_2014_300K`, CC BY) for the exception list and
the abbreviations; wty ga-en, ga-ru, ga-fr and ga-pl (2026.10.22) as the dictionaries.

- Exception list: `mutation-exceptions.mjs ga <sentences> 1000 wty-ga-en.zip wty-ga-ru.zip wty-ga-fr.zip
  wty-ga-pl.zip`, reviewed by hand. Listed 10: chun, thar, thart, thíos, chomh, cheana, chuaigh, chuile (own sense,
  wrong radical: cun, tar, tart, tíos, comh, ceana, cuaigh, cuile), bhfuil (the rule's fuil "blood" is an extra
  entry; bhfuil's own rows lead to bí) and English the (→ te). Left to the rule as right mutations: bhí → bí, i
  ndiaidh → diaidh, na hÉireann → Éireann and the rest.
- Shares of tokens, without the list → with it: ga-en extra entries 13.44% → 11.99%; found by the rule alone with
  ga-ru 3.18% → 3.05%, ga-fr 5.56% → 4.84%, ga-pl 3.08% → 3.02%.
- Rows: ga-en writes `eclipsis` 19,816, `lenition` 17,867, `lenited form` 1,962, `prothesis-h` 272 and `lenition
  mutation` once (ṡlógacallam → slógacallam), and `triggers-*` tags on the words that cause a mutation (not form
  rows); ga-fr `radical` 13 and `radical lenition` once, from a radical to its mutated forms, and `lenition` twice;
  ga-ru and ga-pl none.
- Abbreviations (before a lowercase word / a capital / a digit / at the end; Leipzig's splitter cuts after many
  abbreviations, so the ends include its cuts): m.sh. 52 / 14 / 2 / 11; m.s. 3 / 1 / 0 / 2; lch. 3 / 0 / 20 / 5; Br.
  2 / 0 / 0 / 0; Dr. 142 at the end; Co. 215 / 0 / 0 / 252 (Co. na Gaillimhe, Co. Chiarraí cut). Before a
  lowercase word only: srl. 35 / 0 / 2 / 263; Teo. 11 / 0 / 0 / 50; i.n. 37 / 24 / 0 / 3; r.n. 17 / 4 / 0 / 2. a.m.
  (9 / 4 / 0 / 11) is also English and left out.
- Elided words: d’ 16,171 and d' 7,663; b’ 3,940 and b' 1,940; m’ 810 and m' 395.
- Catalog: wty ga-en 21,903 words, ga-ru 3,848, ga-fr 2,303, ga-pl 2,110 and the transcription (11,828) are listed
  and ticked; ga-es 1,149, ga-zh 838, ga-pt 816, ga-ko 588, ga-tr 481, ga-ja 435, ga-de 254, ga-it 148 and ga-vi 127
  stay under the line.
- Recognition, audio, fonts: the plan's measurements (`plans/2026-10-19-group-5b.md`, Research).

### Scottish Gaelic (2026-10-28)

Leipzig has no Gaelic corpus, so the text of the Gaelic Wikipedia's articles (dump gdwiki-20261001, 36,615
paragraphs, 1.58 million words, CC BY-SA; markup stripped: templates, references, tables, files, categories, lines
under 40 characters; kept in `testdata/corpus/gd/`) served for the exception list and the abbreviations; wty gd-en
and gd-ru (2026.10.22) as the dictionaries.

- Exception list: `mutation-exceptions.mjs gd <text> 1000 wty-gd-en.zip wty-gd-ru.zip`: 21 not attested, none own
  and common, 106 left to the rule. Listed 7 by hand: bhathar (was, not bathar "goods"), bheir (will give, not beir
  "catch"), dhuibh (to you, not duibh "black"), shuas (up, the place, not suas, the motion), chan (not, not can
  "say"), dha (to him, not da) and English the. Left to the rule as right mutations or as words leading back to
  themselves: fhèin → fèin, thug → tug → thoir, dheth → deth → dheth, bhaile → baile, h-Alba → Alba and the rest.
- Shares of tokens, without the list → with it: gd-en extra entries 5.39% → 5.16%; gd-ru 0.02% (unchanged).
- Rows: gd-en writes `lenition mutation` 3,170, `lenited form` 437, `eclipsis mutation`, `mutation prothesis-h` and
  `mutation prothesis-t` 594 each (one row each for every word starting with a vowel), `mutation mutation-radical`
  104 (cill → chill, from the radical), `lenition` 2, `eclipsis` 3 (two from Irish-style tables: bhfigh → figh),
  `prothesis-h` 3, and `triggers-*` tags on the words that cause a mutation (not form rows); gd-ru none.
- Abbreviations (before a capital / a lowercase word / at the end): m.e. 61 / 78 / 51; Urr. 33 / 0 / 0; Dr. 15 / 0
  / 0; Mgr. 8 / 0 / 2; Oll. 4 / 0 / 0. msaa. 4 / 0 / 5 and msa. 4 / 0 / 0, which close a list and often a sentence,
  only before a lowercase word.
- Elided words: dh' 4,731 (dh'àirde, dh'aois, dh'fhàs), th' 832 (th'ann 784), bh' 301 (bh'ann 259), d' 212
  (d'fhuair), b' 173 (b'àbhaist, b'e), m' 3 (encyclopedic text has little dialogue; m'athair is common in other
  text).
- Catalog: wty gd-en 9,725 words, gd-ru 1,760 and the transcription (4,437) are listed and ticked; gd-fr 1,083,
  gd-pt 987, gd-es 372, gd-zh 313, gd-tr 308, gd-ja 260 and gd-vi 59 stay under the line. The catalog was
  regenerated for Gaelic only: the other entries wty changed since (333, and he-tr) are left for their own refresh.
- Recognition: the plan's measurements (`plans/2026-10-19-group-5b.md`, Research): ML Kit 0.65% of letters wrong,
  Tesseract 0.29% (unmarked: less than half a point better), PaddleOCR v5 0.07% and v6 none (marked more accurate).

## Device checks

`test-model.md`: LK28 (stress and tone marks typed apart), LK29 (rock’n’roll), OCR26 (app text across a wrap),
ANK12 (`{sentence}` edge cases: ３．１４, LTD. ŞTİ., German text with English quotes), ANK13 (sentence furigana in a
language looked up by words), ANK14 (furigana markers by language, their hints, Russian stress in the text), LK30
(a word hyphenated at a line end), ANK12 extended (Plan B., ok., English quotes in German), POP24 (the Turkish chip
initial), LK37 (Ukrainian), LK38 (Belarusian), LK39 (Greek), LK42 (Hindi), LK43 (Bengali), LK44 (Georgian), LK45 (Armenian), LK46 (Thai), LK47 (Khmer), LNG17 (the busy-screen line of Thai and Khmer), LK48 (Arabic), LK49 (Persian, with its spoken endings), LK50 (Hebrew), LK51 (Kazakh), LK52 (the parts of a hyphenated word), LK36 and LK50 (no
form tags beside a plain word), POP27 (Persian transcription order), LK54 (Arabic written with the Persian ک ی), ANK22 (Czech "n. l." from OCR), LK72 (Esperanto without hats and in the x-system), ANK34 (Esperanto's “ closing „…“ and
opening “…”), DIC39 (Esperanto's frequency), LK73 (Albanian endings and elisions),
ANK35 (Albanian abbreviations and quotes), DIC40 (Albanian's frequency), LK74 (Swahili prefixes), ANK36 (Swahili
abbreviations and quotes), DIC41 (Swahili's frequency), LK75 (Welsh mutations, the unmarked fallback and the
OCR-only ý), OCR45 (Welsh ŵ ŷ by engine), ANK37 (Welsh abbreviations), LK76 (Irish mutations, the exception list,
elisions and Gaelic type), OCR46 (Irish fada and prefixes by engine), ANK38 (Irish abbreviations), LK77 (Scottish
Gaelic mutations, the exception list, the acute as grave and the unmarked fallback), OCR47 (Gaelic grave accents and
hyphenated prefixes by engine), ANK39 (Gaelic abbreviations), LK78 (Finnish clitics, possessives, colon and hyphen
endings, compounds), DIC42 (the Finnish word forms item), ANK40 (Finnish abbreviations and ordinals), OCR48 (Finnish
ä ö, colons and hyphens by engine), POP46 (the Finnish font card and the chips in Russian), AUD33 (Finnish audio
sources), LNG32 (the Finnish download screen), LK79 (Hungarian verb prefixes, ő ű from OCR, hyphen endings,
compounds), DIC43 (the Hungarian word forms item), ANK41 (Hungarian abbreviations and dates), OCR49 (Hungarian ő ű
by engine), POP47 (the Hungarian font card and the form tags in Russian), AUD34 (Hungarian audio sources), LNG33 (the
Hungarian download screen).
